# France Wage Type Catalogue (WTC) - FR-WTC-001 v1.0

Every row of the delivered France wage type catalogue: 300 Global WTC baseline codes plus 74 France-specific FR- rows (374 total), with tax and social-security treatment flags, accumulators, formula text and tax codes. Includes the Sick Leave Paid By Employer bands, the Tax Credit sheet, the Global WTC coverage map and the Form Reference Guide.


## Provenance

| Source artifact | Bytes | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|
| `FR-compliance/02-wtc/FR-WTC-001-v1.0.xlsx` | 455,655 | `3ab147982476b7dc…` |

This file is a complete, verbatim conversion of the artifacts above. Figures, dates, code values, French terminology and legal citations are reproduced exactly as delivered; nothing is summarised or rounded.

# France Wage Type Catalogue - all sheets

## Sheet: `Version Control`

_27 rows x 6 columns._

**Wage Type Catalogue**

**France (FR) - income year 2026**

| col1 | Version | Effective Date | Change Description | Status | Updated By |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|  | 1.0 | 2026-08-27T00:00:00 | Initial France Wage Type Catalogue. Built from the CZ V2.0 template (loaded, data rows cleared, formatting preserved), from FR-CCG-001-v1.0 (13 modules) and from the Global WTC V1.0 baseline. ALL 300 global Pay Element Codes configured for France in baseline order, plus 74 France-specific wage types. 2026 parameters throughout: monthly social security ceiling EUR 4,005 / annual EUR 48,060; SMIC EUR 12.02 to 31 May and EUR 12.31 from 1 June, with the general reduction reference FROZEN at EUR 12.02 for the whole year; unified degressive general reduction replacing the abolished reduced health and family-allowance rates. | Draft | Wallisson Gomes |
|  | REMARKS |  |  |  |  |
|  | Topic | Remark |  |  |  |
|  | SOCIAL SECURITY STRUCTURE - COLUMN Q EXPANDED INTO FOUR PER-FUND COLUMNS | France has NO single social security contribution. An ordinary private-sector payslip carries between eighteen and twenty-four separate levies collected by THREE NETWORKS - URSSAF for the general scheme and most attached levies, the Agirc-Arrco supplementary pension institutions, and the insurers of the compulsory health and death-and-disability cover - running on FOUR DISTINCT BASES that never coincide. The CZ template's single "Subject to Social Insurance" column cannot express this, so it has been expanded per the v2.4 SS FUND EXPANSION instruction into: Q1 Subject to URSSAF General Scheme (the Article L242-1 base: health 13.00%, capped old-age 6.90% / 8.55%, uncapped old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, AT-MP at the establishment rate, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, FNAL, social dialogue 0.016%, mobility levy); Q2 Subject to CSG / CRDS (the Article L136-1-1 base: 98.25% of pay to four annual ceilings then 100%, PLUS employer cover contributions at 100% with no allowance); Q3 Subject to Unemployment Insurance and AGS (4.00% + 0.25% to four monthly ceilings, EUR 16,020); Q4 Subject to the Compulsory Health and Death-and-Disability Cover (mutuelle and prevoyance, inside the shared Article D242-1 envelope). Column R (Subject to Pension) covers the basic old-age scheme AND Agirc-Arrco bands 1 and 2, the general balance contribution, the technical balance contribution and Apec. THE SINGLE MOST IMPORTANT CONSEQUENCE: "subject to contributions" (Q1) and "subject to CSG" (Q2) ARE NOT THE SAME TEST, and dozens of rows in this catalogue answer them differently - profit-sharing, incentive schemes, savings-plan top-ups, the value-sharing bonus and the employer cover contributions are the clearest cases. |  |  |  |
|  | COLUMN SEMANTICS USED IN THIS CATALOGUE | N Subject to Tax: Yes = the amount enters the remuneration nette fiscale and is therefore inside the withholding-at-source base; No = statutorily excluded; Yes-No = partly in and partly out, where the split depends on a limit or on a case-by-case determination payroll cannot pre-decide; NA = not an income line. \| O Table Tax: Yes = the ordinary withholding-at-source rate applies - the personal or individualised rate transmitted by the tax administration, or the statutory default grid; No = it does not; NA = not an income line. FRANCE HAS NO PROGRESSIVE PAYROLL SCALE and no separate bonus schedule, so "Table Tax" here means only "does the transmitted rate or the default grid bite". \| P Special Tax: Yes = a levy or a withholding OTHER than the ordinary withholding at source applies - the Article 182 A non-resident withholding, the 40% contribution on a mutually agreed termination or a compulsory retirement, the 8% / 20% social lump-sum levy, the 30% employer contribution on free shares or share options, the 10% employee contribution on a free share acquisition gain, or the payroll tax. \| Q1-Q4 and R: Yes = fully in that base; No = outside it; Yes-No = inside within a limit and outside beyond it, or turning on a per-case test; NA = not an income line. |  |  |  |
|  | WHERE THE "Yes-No" VALUE IS USED, AND WHERE ITS JUSTIFICATION LIVES | The v2.5 C3 checklist requires every Yes-No in a core taxability column to carry an explanation. COLUMN D (Wage Type Explanation) IS AN ORANGE COLUMN and this build populates NO orange column, so the justification is carried in COLUMN F (Formula), which is BLUE and is expressly intended to hold plain text, behind the literal marker "YES-NO JUSTIFICATION:". selfcheck_fr_wtc.py enforces this mechanically: every row carrying Yes-No in N, O, P, Q, R, S, T or U must contain that marker in column F, and the run FAILS if one does not. The families using Yes-No are: the termination indemnities, where three separate exemption tests with three different ceilings apply to one payment; the employer cover contributions, exempt within the Article D242-1 envelope and reintegrated above it; the flat professional-expense allowances, exempt within their own limit and remuneration above it; the apprentice and intern pay, exempt to a cumulative annual threshold; and the items whose treatment turns on who pays (employer or works council) or on whether an outlay is evidenced. |  |  |  |
|  | TAX CODE COLUMN - SOURCE AND THE C9 REQUIREMENT | France has no per-wage-type tax box of the Belgian 281.10 or Czech kind. The functional equivalent is the DSN NEODeS rubrique and its code value, which is what column G carries: the remuneration type (S21.G00.51.011), the bonus / indemnity type (S21.G00.52.001), the other gross-income type (S21.G00.54.001), the contribution base code (S21.G00.78.001), the individual contribution code (S21.G00.81.001) or the withholding rubriques of S21.G00.50. EVERY CODE IS SOURCED IN COLUMN X against the DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1 held at FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf, whose code lists were parsed into ct2026_rubriques.json in the same folder and read directly by this build. NO CODE WAS TAKEN FROM ANOTHER COUNTRY'S LEGEND AND NONE WAS GUESSED: where a wage type is an internal payroll line with no coded rubrique of its own, column G is "NA" and column X states that no code is asserted and which code lists were checked to establish it. |  |  |  |
|  | BLUE / ORANGE CONVENTION APPLIED, AND WHY COLUMN A IS POPULATED | POPULATED (BLUE): B, C, E, F, G, J, N, O, P, Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4, R and the new X. LEFT DELIBERATELY EMPTY FOR THE DEVELOPMENT TEAM (ORANGE): D Wage Type Explanation, H Calculation Type, I Prorating Method, K Ignore If 0, L Enforce Setting, M In Period Reconciliation, V YTD Load, W YTD Remarks. COLUMN A (Mercans Code) IS ORANGE AND IS NEVERTHELESS POPULATED, following the delivered Belgium precedent: the orange fill marks development-team ownership of the FINAL value, not that the column must stay empty, and without it there is no direct link from a row to the Global WTC baseline. The Global WTC Coverage sheet carries the same traceability independently, so the link survives even if a reviewer clears column A. |  |  |  |
|  | PARAMETERS THAT MOVE DURING 2026 - CONFIGURE AS DATED VALUES, NOT CONSTANTS | THREE DIFFERENT WAGE REFERENCES ARE SIMULTANEOUSLY LIVE. (1) SMIC, live: EUR 12.02 per hour to 31 May 2026, EUR 12.31 from 1 June (EUR 1,823.03 then EUR 1,867.02 per month at 35 x 52 / 12 = 151.6666... hours - NOT 151.67, which is off by four cents every year and produces spurious minimum-wage failures). (2) SMIC, FROZEN at EUR 12.02 for the whole of 2026 for the unified degressive general reduction alone, by Decret n. 2026-509 of 12 June 2026: an engine reading a single "current minimum wage" variable will silently overstate the relief for seven months. (3) Social security ceiling, from the average wage per head: EUR 4,005 monthly and EUR 48,060 annual, to be taken from the Arrete of 22 December 2025 and never computed from the announced percentage. Also dated: the withholding default grid changes ON 1 MAY 2026 and BOTH grids must be held, the applicable one decided by the DATE THE INCOME IS PAID; the maximum daily sickness benefit moves from EUR 41.95 to EUR 42.97 on 1 July 2026; the short-contract allowance moves from EUR 748 to EUR 766 on 1 June 2026; the guaranteed minimum moves from EUR 4.22 to EUR 4.35 on 1 June 2026; the ENHANCED VALUE-SHARING BONUS REGIME EXPIRES ON 31 DECEMBER 2026 and must be carried into the 2027 configuration review; and the sector-specific flat deduction percentage falls by one point a year toward abolition (24% for journalists in 2026). |  |  |  |
|  | THE 2026 EMPLOYER-SIDE STRUCTURAL CHANGE | The reduced employer rates for health (7.00% instead of 13.00%) and family allowances (3.45% instead of 5.25%) for employees below defined multiples of the minimum wage ARE REPEALED FROM 1 JANUARY 2026. Every employer now applies the full 13.00% and 5.25%, and the value of the former reduced bands is delivered instead through the UNIFIED DEGRESSIVE GENERAL REDUCTION (FR-024) as a separate calculated deduction. An engine carrying a 2025 reduced-rate band will under-charge every low-paid employee. The reduction itself has a CLIFF OF TWO PERCENTAGE POINTS at three times the frozen annual reference (EUR 65,629.20), not a taper to zero - modelling a smooth decay understates the relief across the whole upper range. |  |  |  |
|  | SCOPE - REGIME GENERAL ONLY | This catalogue is scoped to the REGIME GENERAL: the standard private-sector employment regime. The regimes speciaux - RATP, ENIM, CNBF, CRPCEN, CAVIMAC, CNIEG and CAMIEG, and any other sector-specific fund outside the regime general - ARE OUT OF SCOPE and no rate, rule or reporting obligation has been configured for them (FR-071). The construction, public works and entertainment sector paid-leave funds are likewise out of scope (FR-069), as are attendance fees paid to non-executive directors (FR-072) and free share and share option gains beyond the two specific contributions that touch the DSN. Scope locked before Stage 1 and recorded in shared/FR_regime_scope_note.txt and in FR-compliance/scope_manifest.json. |  |  |  |
|  | ITEMS RECORDED AS EXPLICIT NEGATIVES | Four rows exist so that a requirement referring to them is RAISED AS A DEFECT rather than built: FR-069 sector paid-leave funds (the employer pays no leave pay at all in those sectors); FR-072 attendance fees (investment income, outside the gross-to-net chain); FR-073 STUDENT LOAN REPAYMENT - NO SUCH STATUTORY DEDUCTION EXISTS IN FRANCE, so a requirement referring to one is an error in the requirement; and FR-074 the Article 197 A MINIMUM ASSESSMENT RATE for non-residents, which operates at ASSESSMENT level and must NOT be modelled in the payroll engine, in this catalogue or in the data dictionary. Wage type 62599 is a further negative: the COVID-era exceptional purchasing-power regime has ended and no exemption may be configured on it for 2026. |  |  |  |
|  | SHEET NAMES RETAINED FROM THE CZ TEMPLATE | The third sheet is delivered under the template's own name "Sick Leave Paid By Employer" rather than renamed to "Employer Sick Pay", because the build instruction is to LOAD the CZ file and preserve its structure rather than to create a new workbook. The content is the France employer sick-pay regime in full. Sheets 5 and 6 - "Global WTC Coverage" and "Form Reference Guide" - are new and are appended after the four template sheets, in the order required by v2.4 C17. |  |  |  |

| 21100 | Basic salary reduction - unpaid absence | Retenue sur salaire de base - absence non remuneree |  | Deduction | Value of the unpaid absence, deducted from the smoothed monthly salary. Labour Code Article L3242-1 makes the monthly salary INDEPENDENT of the number of days in the month, so nothing is prorated for the calendar alone. The absence is valued with the REAL-HOURS method - monthly salary x hours not worked / hours the employee should have worked that month - which is the method preferred by the courts because it never penalises the employee for the shape of the calendar. Calendar days are used only for a hire or a departure, and the monthly social security ceiling MUST then be prorated by the SAME method (4,005.00 x calendar days of employment / calendar days in the month). Using working days for the salary and calendar days for the ceiling produces a wrong capped band every time. Reduces every contribution base, the CSG / CRDS base and the net taxable amount of the period. The absence is declared in the DSN activity block as '02 - Duree d'absence partiellement ou pas du tout remuneree' with its measure and unit. | S21.G00.53.001 / 02 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.53.001 (Type d'activite ou d'inactivite), code 02 - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 21102 | Basic salary adjustment - correction within the current year | Regularisation du salaire de base - correction sur l'annee en cours |  | Deduction | Correction of the base salary of an earlier month of the SAME calendar year. Correction within the same calendar year. Added to (or deducted from) the current month's bases at the current rates for income tax, because withholding at source is not cumulative and no true-up is performed in payroll. For social security the attachment period is carried in the DSN block so that the collecting body allocates the correction to the month it belongs to, which re-opens that month's ceiling reconciliation and its share of the unified degressive general reduction. Declared under the ordinary base-salary remuneration type with the corrected period's dates. | S21.G00.51.011 / 010 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 21103 | Recovery of a salary advance (avance), one tenth of pay | Retenue de l'avance sur salaire (1/10e de la remuneration) |  | Deduction | Recovery of an advance in the strict sense - a sum lent against future work. Recovery of an advance in the strict sense (avance). Labour Code Article L3251-3 caps the recovery at ONE TENTH of the pay due at each period. Taken from net pay AFTER all contributions and after the withholding at source, so it changes no base and no declared amount. It ranks LAST - behind the absolutely protected amount (EUR 651.69 per month for 2026), maintenance orders paid by direct payment, tax levies and ordinary garnishments; any balance not recoverable within the tenth is carried forward and is never written off. It is NOT the acompte of FR-063, which is a payment on account of the SAME month's salary and is netted in full without any limit. Confusing the two is the classic French error: the tenth applies to the avance only. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. | 21108 | Salary reduction - sickness absence | Retenue sur salaire - absence pour maladie |  | Deduction | Deduction of the sickness absence from the smoothed salary, as step 1 of the three-line sickness payslip (deduct the absence, add the daily social security benefit where the employer is subrogated, add the employer top-up). Labour Code Article L3242-1 makes the monthly salary INDEPENDENT of the number of days in the month, so nothing is prorated for the calendar alone. The absence is valued with the REAL-HOURS method - monthly salary x hours not worked / hours the employee should have worked that month - which is the method preferred by the courts because it never penalises the employee for the shape of the calendar. Calendar days are used only for a hire or a departure, and the monthly social security ceiling MUST then be prorated by the SAME method (4,005.00 x calendar days of employment / calendar days in the month). Using working days for the salary and calendar days for the ceiling produces a wrong capped band every time. Declared as activity type '02' and, separately, in the work-stoppage block S21.G00.60 which carries the last day worked, the subrogation flag and the bank details. | S21.G00.53.001 / 02 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.53.001 (Type d'activite ou d'inactivite), code 02 - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 21109 | Salary reduction - unpaid leave of absence | Retenue sur salaire - conge sans solde |  | Deduction | Unpaid leave suspends the employment contract without ending it. No contribution is due because there is no pay; the monthly ceiling is prorated to the days actually covered by pay. Labour Code Article L3242-1 makes the monthly salary INDEPENDENT of the number of days in the month, so nothing is prorated for the calendar alone. The absence is valued with the REAL-HOURS method - monthly salary x hours not worked / hours the employee should have worked that month - which is the method preferred by the courts because it never penalises the employee for the shape of the calendar. Calendar days are used only for a hire or a departure, and the monthly social security ceiling MUST then be prorated by the SAME method (4,005.00 x calendar days of employment / calendar days in the month). Using working days for the salary and calendar days for the ceiling produces a wrong capped band every time. Compulsory health cover normally CONTINUES during an unpaid suspension unless the founding document provides otherwise, and the employer's share then remains a CSG / CRDS base and a net taxable item even in a month with no cash salary - the single most-missed consequence of unpaid leave in France. | S21.G00.53.001 / 02 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.53.001 (Type d'activite ou d'inactivite), code 02 - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 21110 | Overtime reduction - 25% band (36th to 43rd hour) | Annulation heures supplementaires majorees a 25 % (36e a 43e heure) |  | Deduction | Reverses overtime paid at the 25% statutory premium of Labour Code Article L3121-36. Negative line reversing an amount already paid or already valued. It carries the SAME social and tax treatment as the wage type it reverses, with the sign inverted, so that every base it entered is reduced by the same amount. Where the reversal relates to a CLOSED month the correction is filed through the DSN correction mechanism for the period concerned, the block carrying that period's dates, never by netting it against the current month's base - Social Security Code Article R243-14 and the DSN cahier technique both require the period of attachment to travel with the amount. The reversal must also REVERSE the three overtime reliefs of wage type 62110 - the 11.31% employee old-age relief, the employer flat deduction per hour and the amount counted against the EUR 7,500 annual income tax exemption. Failing to unwind the EUR 7,500 counter is the most common defect on an overtime correction. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

Where the reversal relates to a CLOSED month the correction is filed through the DSN correction mechanism for the period concerned, the block carrying that period's dates, never by netting it against the current month's base - Social Security Code Article R243-14 and the DSN cahier technique both require the period of attachment to travel with the amount. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

Additional hours may NEVER bring the employee to the full-time duration: if they do, the contract is reclassified as full-time and the reclassification is retroactive. | 21119 | Reduction - days beyond the annual day-count arrangement | Annulation jours travailles au-dela du forfait annuel en jours |  | Deduction | Reverses days paid beyond the agreed annual day count (typically 218 days). Employees on a day-count arrangement are OUTSIDE the hours framework and have no overtime; days beyond the count, with the employer's agreement, carry a premium of at least 10%. The employer flat deduction is seven times the hourly amount per additional day, and reversing the day must reverse seven times the hourly deduction, not one. Negative line reversing an amount already paid or already valued. | 21149 | Requalification of an unjustified allowance as remuneration | Requalification en salaire d'une indemnite non justifiee |  | Deduction | Not a reversal but a REQUALIFICATION. An allowance presented as an expense reimbursement that cannot be justified under the Arrete of 20 December 2002 - no evidence, no genuine business purpose, or a flat amount above the statutory limit with no supporting rule - is remuneration IN FULL from the first euro, not merely on the excess. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. Regularised through a corrective DSN for the original period, with late-payment interest. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

Only the 100% premium for work on 1 MAY is statutory (Labour Code Article L3133-6); every other premium of this family comes from the agreement and must be configured per IDCC. Negative line reversing an amount already paid or already valued. It carries the SAME social and tax treatment as the wage type it reverses, with the sign inverted, so that every base it entered is reduced by the same amount. Where the reversal relates to a CLOSED month the correction is filed through the DSN correction mechanism for the period concerned, the block carrying that period's dates, never by netting it against the current month's base - Social Security Code Article R243-14 and the DSN cahier technique both require the period of attachment to travel with the amount. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 21190 | Salary reduction - long-term incapacity and invalidity | Retenue sur salaire - incapacite de longue duree et invalidite |  | Deduction | Applied once the employer's top-up obligation under Labour Code Article L1226-1 is exhausted - 90% of the pay the employee would have received for 30 days, then 66.66% for the next 30, each period extended by 10 days per five years of service beyond the first, to a maximum of 90 days at each rate. Beyond that, income replacement passes to the sickness insurance fund and, on recognition of invalidity, to the invalidity pension; any employer or scheme supplement paid afterwards is a separate wage type (62190). Labour Code Article L3242-1 makes the monthly salary INDEPENDENT of the number of days in the month, so nothing is prorated for the calendar alone. The absence is valued with the REAL-HOURS method - monthly salary x hours not worked / hours the employee should have worked that month - which is the method preferred by the courts because it never penalises the employee for the shape of the calendar. Calendar days are used only for a hire or a departure, and the monthly social security ceiling MUST then be prorated by the SAME method (4,005.00 x calendar days of employment / calendar days in the month). Using working days for the salary and calendar days for the ceiling produces a wrong capped band every time. | S21.G00.53.001 / 02 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.53.001 (Type d'activite ou d'inactivite), code 02 - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 21210 | Reversal of the employee overtime old-age relief | Annulation de la reduction de cotisations salariales sur heures supplementaires |  | Deduction | Reverses the employee old-age contribution relief of Social Security Code Article L241-17 where the underlying hours are cancelled or requalified. The relief equals the employee's EFFECTIVE statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31%; where the effective rate is lower - a part-time employee entirely inside band 1, for example - the lower rate applies and the reversal must use the SAME rate that was granted, not the cap. | S21.G00.81.001 / 114 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

The deduction is capped at the employer contributions actually due, so a reversal can re-open capacity that was previously lost to the cap. | S21.G00.81.001 / 021 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 21610 | Employee share - compulsory supplementary health cover | Part salariale de la complementaire sante obligatoire (mutuelle) |  | Deduction | Employee share of the compulsory supplementary health cover that Labour Code Article L911-7 imposes on every private-sector employer, the employer bearing AT LEAST 50% of the premium. | 62100 | Base salary | Salaire de base |  | Earning | Contractual annual salary / 12, smoothed across the year under Labour Code Article L3242-1 so that the monthly amount is INDEPENDENT of the number of working days in the month. Full-time reference is 151.6666... hours per month (35 x 52 / 12), and the hourly rate for every other purpose is the monthly salary / 151.67. Part-time: full-time monthly salary x contractual hours / 151.67. The binding floor is the HIGHER of the statutory minimum wage and the applicable collective-agreement minimum for the employee's grade - EUR 12.02 per hour to 31 May 2026 and EUR 12.31 from 1 June, giving EUR 1,823.03 and EUR 1,867.02 per month. Note that the monthly minimum is the hourly rate x the exact fraction 151.6666..., NOT x 151.67: 12.31 x 151.6666 = 1,867.02 against 1,867.06, and a minimum-wage test built on 151.67 produces spurious near-threshold failures every year. Declared as NEODeS type '010 - Salaire de base', which EXCLUDES every salary supplement - seniority bonus, holiday bonus, thirteenth month, family-related bonuses, benefits in cash and in kind, gratuities, tips and occasional overtime or additional hours. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 010 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62101 | Base salary arrears | Rappel de salaire de base |  | Earning | Base salary relating to a closed period and paid now. ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. Declared under the base-salary type with the attachment period carried in the DSN so that the collecting body allocates it to the month of work. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). Employees on a day-count arrangement (typically 218 days) are OUTSIDE the hours framework entirely and have no overtime; days worked beyond the agreed count, with the employer's agreement, carry a premium of at least 10%. THE EMPLOYER FLAT DEDUCTION IS SEVEN TIMES THE HOURLY AMOUNT PER ADDITIONAL DAY - EUR 10.50 below 20 employees and EUR 3.50 at 20 or more - not one hour's worth. OVERTIME RELIEFS, all three of which apply: (1) employee old-age contribution relief equal to the employee's effective statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31% (= 6.90% + 0.40% basic old-age, 3.15% Agirc-Arrco band 1 and 0.86% general balance contribution band 1), Social Security Code Article L241-17; (2) employer flat deduction of EUR 1.50 per overtime hour below 20 employees and EUR 0.50 at 20 or more, available to employers of EVERY size from 1 January 2026 (Article 21 of Act n. 2025-1403 of 30 December 2025 amending Article L241-18-1), capped at the employer contributions actually due; (3) income tax exemption of base rate and premium together up to EUR 7,500 NET per employee per calendar year, cumulative across employers, General Tax Code Article 81 quater. THE RELIEFS DO NOT TOUCH CSG / CRDS: overtime remains fully subject to the 9.70%. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62121 | Structural overtime | Heures supplementaires structurelles |  | Earning | Overtime built into the contractual working time itself - a 39-hour contract carrying four structural overtime hours a week. NEODeS gives it its own type '018' distinct from occasional overtime '017'; declaring it under the wrong type produces a filing anomaly even where the amount is correct. The reliefs apply on the same terms. OVERTIME RELIEFS, all three of which apply: (1) employee old-age contribution relief equal to the employee's effective statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31% (= 6.90% + 0.40% basic old-age, 3.15% Agirc-Arrco band 1 and 0.86% general balance contribution band 1), Social Security Code Article L241-17; (2) employer flat deduction of EUR 1.50 per overtime hour below 20 employees and EUR 0.50 at 20 or more, available to employers of EVERY size from 1 January 2026 (Article 21 of Act n. 2025-1403 of 30 December 2025 amending Article L241-18-1), capped at the employer contributions actually due; (3) income tax exemption of base rate and premium together up to EUR 7,500 NET per employee per calendar year, cumulative across employers, General Tax Code Article 81 quater. OVERTIME RELIEFS, all three of which apply: (1) employee old-age contribution relief equal to the employee's effective statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31% (= 6.90% + 0.40% basic old-age, 3.15% Agirc-Arrco band 1 and 0.86% general balance contribution band 1), Social Security Code Article L241-17; (2) employer flat deduction of EUR 1.50 per overtime hour below 20 employees and EUR 0.50 at 20 or more, available to employers of EVERY size from 1 January 2026 (Article 21 of Act n. 2025-1403 of 30 December 2025 amending Article L241-18-1), capped at the employer contributions actually due; (3) income tax exemption of base rate and premium together up to EUR 7,500 NET per employee per calendar year, cumulative across employers, General Tax Code Article 81 quater. | 62123 | Equivalent hours | Heures d'equivalence |  | Earning | Hours paid under a sector equivalence regime, where a longer presence is treated as equal to the statutory duration - road transport, private hospitals, residential care. NEODeS type '012'. THEY ARE NOT OVERTIME and attract NONE of the three overtime reliefs; they are ordinary remuneration. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. | 62133 | Overtime arrears | Rappel d'heures supplementaires |  | Earning | Overtime relating to a closed period. ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. The three reliefs are computed at the parameters OF THE PERIOD OF WORK for the social reliefs and against the CURRENT year's EUR 7,500 cap for the income tax exemption, because that cap is a calendar-year cap keyed on the year of PAYMENT. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62134 | Overtime adjustment | Regularisation d'heures supplementaires |  | Earning | Correction of overtime of an earlier month of the same calendar year. Correction within the same calendar year. Added to (or deducted from) the current month's bases at the current rates for income tax, because withholding at source is not cumulative and no true-up is performed in payroll. For social security the attachment period is carried in the DSN block so that the collecting body allocates the correction to the month it belongs to, which re-opens that month's ceiling reconciliation and its share of the unified degressive general reduction. The employee old-age relief, the employer flat deduction and the EUR 7,500 counter are all adjusted with it. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

Apprenticeship pay is exempt from income tax up to the ANNUAL MINIMUM WAGE per calendar year (General Tax Code Article 81 bis), and overtime pay counts inside that threshold before the separate EUR 7,500 overtime exemption is reached - the two counters are distinct and both must be maintained. OVERTIME RELIEFS, all three of which apply: (1) employee old-age contribution relief equal to the employee's effective statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31% (= 6.90% + 0.40% basic old-age, 3.15% Agirc-Arrco band 1 and 0.86% general balance contribution band 1), Social Security Code Article L241-17; (2) employer flat deduction of EUR 1.50 per overtime hour below 20 employees and EUR 0.50 at 20 or more, available to employers of EVERY size from 1 January 2026 (Article 21 of Act n. 2025-1403 of 30 December 2025 amending Article L241-18-1), capped at the employer contributions actually due; (3) income tax exemption of base rate and premium together up to EUR 7,500 NET per employee per calendar year, cumulative across employers, General Tax Code Article 81 quater. THE RELIEFS DO NOT TOUCH CSG / CRDS: overtime remains fully subject to the 9.70%. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: SUBJECT TO TAX is Yes-No because the apprentice's annual minimum-wage income tax exemption covers the overtime pay until that cumulative threshold is crossed, after which the pay enters the net taxable amount in full; the boundary depends on the individual apprentice's year-to-date position, not on the wage type. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62137 | Managerial-grade overtime (cadre integre) | Heures supplementaires du cadre integre |  | Earning | Overtime of a managerial-grade employee who is integrated into a working-time schedule and is therefore inside the hours framework. A senior executive (cadre dirigeant) within Labour Code Article L3111-2 is outside the working-time rules altogether and HAS NO OVERTIME; a cadre on a day-count arrangement uses wage type 62120 instead. The grade is fixed by the collective agreement's classification level, not by job title or pay. OVERTIME RELIEFS, all three of which apply: (1) employee old-age contribution relief equal to the employee's effective statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31% (= 6.90% + 0.40% basic old-age, 3.15% Agirc-Arrco band 1 and 0.86% general balance contribution band 1), Social Security Code Article L241-17; (2) employer flat deduction of EUR 1.50 per overtime hour below 20 employees and EUR 0.50 at 20 or more, available to employers of EVERY size from 1 January 2026 (Article 21 of Act n. 2025-1403 of 30 December 2025 amending Article L241-18-1), capped at the employer contributions actually due; (3) income tax exemption of base rate and premium together up to EUR 7,500 NET per employee per calendar year, cumulative across employers, General Tax Code Article 81 quater. THE RELIEFS DO NOT TOUCH CSG / CRDS: overtime remains fully subject to the 9.70%. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62138 | Work on the weekly rest day | Heures travaillees pendant le repos hebdomadaire |  | Earning | Hours worked in breach of, or by derogation from, the minimum weekly rest of 24 consecutive hours plus the 11-hour daily rest, that is 35 hours (Labour Code Article L3132-2). Paid as overtime and, where the derogation is irregular, exposing the employer to a separate labour-law sanction that payroll does not model. OVERTIME RELIEFS, all three of which apply: (1) employee old-age contribution relief equal to the employee's effective statutory and compulsory supplementary old-age rate applied to the overtime pay, CAPPED AT 11.31% (= 6.90% + 0.40% basic old-age, 3.15% Agirc-Arrco band 1 and 0.86% general balance contribution band 1), Social Security Code Article L241-17; (2) employer flat deduction of EUR 1.50 per overtime hour below 20 employees and EUR 0.50 at 20 or more, available to employers of EVERY size from 1 January 2026 (Article 21 of Act n. 2025-1403 of 30 December 2025 amending Article L241-18-1), capped at the employer contributions actually due; (3) income tax exemption of base rate and premium together up to EUR 7,500 NET per employee per calendar year, cumulative across employers, General Tax Code Article 81 quater. THE RELIEFS DO NOT TOUCH CSG / CRDS: overtime remains fully subject to the 9.70%. | S21.G00.51.011 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62139 | Work on 1 May - 100% statutory premium | Majoration de 100 % pour travail le 1er mai |  | Earning | Labour Code Article L3133-6: an employee required to work on 1 May receives, IN ADDITION to the pay for the day, an indemnity equal to that pay - a 100% premium, and the only statutory public holiday premium in France. Ordinary remuneration in every respect; it is NOT overtime and attracts none of the overtime reliefs unless the hours also exceed the weekly duration. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62140 | Seniority bonus | Prime d'anciennete |  | Earning | Grade minimum (or base salary, per the applicable agreement) x the seniority percentage for completed years of service. Not statutory: it is MANDATORY wherever the sector agreement provides for it, and both the percentage scale and the base it applies to are IDCC-level parameters. | 62141 | Seniority bonus arrears | Rappel de prime d'anciennete |  | Earning | Seniority bonus for a closed period, typically after a retrospective recognition of service or a grade reclassification. ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62142 | Seniority bonus adjustment | Regularisation de prime d'anciennete |  | Earning | Correction within the same calendar year. Correction within the same calendar year. Added to (or deducted from) the current month's bases at the current rates for income tax, because withholding at source is not cumulative and no true-up is performed in payroll. CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. | 62160 | Paid annual leave pay | Indemnite de conges payes |  | Earning | THE EMPLOYEE MUST RECEIVE THE HIGHER OF TWO AMOUNTS, compared leave by leave. This is a statutory rule, not an employer option, and it is the single most commonly mis-implemented rule in French payroll. A = one-tenth rule = (total gross pay of the reference period x 1/10) x (days of leave taken / days of leave acquired). B = salary maintenance rule = (monthly salary / working days in the month) x days of leave taken. Leave pay = max(A, B). The one-tenth base INCLUDES base salary, overtime, commission, bonuses that are consideration for work and benefits in kind, and EXCLUDES reimbursed expenses, bonuses covering the whole year including the leave period such as a thirteenth month, and profit-sharing. Accrual is 2.5 working days per month of actual work, capped at 30; non-occupational sick leave accrues 2 days per month capped at 24 per reference period since the Act of 22 April 2024, and the Court of Cassation held on 21 January 2026 that the 24-day cap is assessed reference period by reference period. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.52.001 / 046 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62161 | Paid annual leave pay arrears | Rappel d'indemnite de conges payes |  | Earning | Leave pay for a closed period, typically after a recalculation of the one-tenth base or after leave carried over from an earlier reference period was finally taken. Carry-over after sickness runs for 15 MONTHS, and that period begins ONLY on the day the employer informs the employee of the entitlement on return to work - the employer bearing the burden of proving it gave that information. Absent proof, THE LEAVE NEVER LAPSES. ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. | 62162 | Paid annual leave pay adjustment | Regularisation d'indemnite de conges payes |  | Earning | Correction within the same calendar year, most often because the max(one-tenth, maintenance) comparison was run on an incomplete reference-period base. | 62180 | Statutory redundancy payment | Indemnite legale de licenciement |  | Earning | Labour Code Articles L1234-9 and R1234-2. Due to an employee on a permanent contract with at least EIGHT MONTHS' continuous service, dismissed for any reason other than serious or gross misconduct. Formula: 1/4 of a month's reference salary per year of service up to and including the tenth, then 1/3 per year from the eleventh; an incomplete year counts in proportion to COMPLETE MONTHS. Length of service is measured to the END OF THE NOTICE PERIOD whether or not the notice is worked. The reference salary is the MORE FAVOURABLE of the twelve-month average and one third of the last three months, with any annual or exceptional bonus paid inside the three-month window counted ONLY IN PROPORTION - a thirteenth month paid in December counts for 3/12 of its value. The comparison is compulsory, not optional. Where the reference window was depressed by sickness, part-time parental leave or partial activity, the reference salary is RECONSTITUTED at the pay the employee would have received. DOUBLED where the dismissal follows a declaration of unfitness arising from a work accident or occupational disease (Article L1226-14). THREE SEPARATE EXEMPTION TESTS APPLY TO THE SAME AMOUNT AND GIVE THREE DIFFERENT ANSWERS - do not compute one and reuse it. (1) Income tax, General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies: exempt up to the HIGHEST of the legal or collectively agreed amount (no cap), twice the previous calendar year's gross, or half the total indemnity - the last two capped at 6 PASS = EUR 288,360. (2) Social security contributions, Article L242-1 III: exempt for the income-tax-exempt fraction, itself capped at 2 PASS = EUR 96,120. (3) CSG / CRDS, Article L136-1-1: exempt only up to the LOWER of the legal or agreed amount and the contribution-exempt fraction; the excess bears 9.70% on 100% of the amount with NO 1.75% allowance, and that levy is entirely non-deductible for income tax (Article 154 quinquies). CLIFF: where the AGGREGATE of all indemnities paid on the termination exceeds 10 PASS = EUR 480,600, every social exemption is lost and the whole package is contributory and CSG / CRDS-liable FROM THE FIRST EURO. Test the aggregate before any of the three tests above. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: Every taxability column is Yes-No because the same payment is partly exempt and partly liable under three DIFFERENT tests with three different ceilings; no single Yes or No can express it. | S21.G00.52.001 / 007 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62181 | Collectively agreed redundancy payment | Indemnite conventionnelle de licenciement |  | Earning | The amount fixed by the sector or company agreement, which applies INSTEAD of the statutory amount where it is higher - and in many sectors it is substantially higher for managerial grades. Declared under NEODeS type '021 - Indemnite conventionnelle (supplementaire aux indemnites legales)' for the part above the statutory amount. THREE SEPARATE EXEMPTION TESTS APPLY TO THE SAME AMOUNT AND GIVE THREE DIFFERENT ANSWERS - do not compute one and reuse it. (1) Income tax, General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies: exempt up to the HIGHEST of the legal or collectively agreed amount (no cap), twice the previous calendar year's gross, or half the total indemnity - the last two capped at 6 PASS = EUR 288,360. (2) Social security contributions, Article L242-1 III: exempt for the income-tax-exempt fraction, itself capped at 2 PASS = EUR 96,120. (3) CSG / CRDS, Article L136-1-1: exempt only up to the LOWER of the legal or agreed amount and the contribution-exempt fraction; the excess bears 9.70% on 100% of the amount with NO 1.75% allowance, and that levy is entirely non-deductible for income tax (Article 154 quinquies). CLIFF: where the AGGREGATE of all indemnities paid on the termination exceeds 10 PASS = EUR 480,600, every social exemption is lost and the whole package is contributory and CSG / CRDS-liable FROM THE FIRST EURO. Test the aggregate before any of the three tests above. Note the asymmetry that catches employers: the part ABOVE the legal or collectively agreed amount is exempt from contributions within the 2 PASS ceiling if it is income-tax exempt, but is SUBJECT TO CSG AND CRDS in full, because the CSG exemption stops at the legal or agreed amount. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: Same reason as wage type 62180 - three tests, three ceilings, one payment. | S21.G00.52.001 / 021 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62182 | Severance under a collective redundancy plan | Indemnite de licenciement versee dans le cadre d'un plan de sauvegarde de l'emploi |  | Earning | Where an employer with 50 or more employees dismisses 10 or more in 30 days, a collective redundancy plan is compulsory. Severance paid under such a plan is FULLY EXEMPT FROM INCOME TAX WITH NO CEILING (General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies, 1). The SOCIAL exemption is NOT unlimited: contributions are exempt to 2 PASS = EUR 96,120, and CSG / CRDS only to the legal or agreed amount. A voluntary retirement indemnity paid under such a plan likewise becomes exempt, which reverses its ordinary treatment. THREE SEPARATE EXEMPTION TESTS APPLY TO THE SAME AMOUNT AND GIVE THREE DIFFERENT ANSWERS - do not compute one and reuse it. (1) Income tax, General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies: exempt up to the HIGHEST of the legal or collectively agreed amount (no cap), twice the previous calendar year's gross, or half the total indemnity - the last two capped at 6 PASS = EUR 288,360. (2) Social security contributions, Article L242-1 III: exempt for the income-tax-exempt fraction, itself capped at 2 PASS = EUR 96,120. (3) CSG / CRDS, Article L136-1-1: exempt only up to the LOWER of the legal or agreed amount and the contribution-exempt fraction; the excess bears 9.70% on 100% of the amount with NO 1.75% allowance, and that levy is entirely non-deductible for income tax (Article 154 quinquies). CLIFF: where the AGGREGATE of all indemnities paid on the termination exceeds 10 PASS = EUR 480,600, every social exemption is lost and the whole package is contributory and CSG / CRDS-liable FROM THE FIRST EURO. Test the aggregate before any of the three tests above. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: SUBJECT TO TAX is No because the income tax exemption is unlimited here, but the social columns remain Yes-No because the 2 PASS and CSG limits still bite. | S21.G00.52.001 / 007 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62190 | Employer and scheme supplement during long-term incapacity | Complement employeur ou de prevoyance en cas d'incapacite de longue duree |  | Earning | Any employer or death-and-disability scheme supplement paid AFTER the employer's statutory top-up obligation under Labour Code Article L1226-1 is exhausted. Where the employer pays it directly it is ORDINARY REMUNERATION IN EVERY RESPECT - the exemption attaching to the statutory daily benefit does not extend to it. Where an insurer pays it under a death-and-disability scheme financed partly by the employer, the sum is a replacement income subject to CSG at the reduced rate and to CRDS, and is taxable; it transits the payslip only where the employer operates the payment. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.54.001 / 06 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62200 | Thirteenth month | Treizieme mois |  | Earning | One additional month of salary, or a fraction of annual salary, paid annually in November or December, in two instalments, or spread monthly. NOT STATUTORY: it is mandatory only where the collective agreement, a company agreement, the contract or an established custom provides for it. Prorated over the CALENDAR YEAR OF SERVICE, not over the month of payment. It does NOT count toward the minimum-wage comparison unless it is paid in monthly instalments. Inside a three-month severance reference window it counts ONLY IN PROPORTION - 3/12 of its value. FRANCE HAS NO SEPARATE BONUS TAX SCHEDULE AND NO SPREADING MECHANISM. A thirteenth month paid in December is taxed in December at the withholding rate then in force. Because that rate is a fixed percentage rather than a progressive scale applied to the month, a large bonus does NOT push the employee into a higher withholding band; any correction happens the following year when the tax administration recalculates the rate from the annual return. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62210 | Statutory profit-sharing paid immediately | Participation versee immediatement au salarie |  | Earning | Statutory profit-sharing (participation) is compulsory for companies with 50 or more employees. Where the employee ELECTS immediate payment rather than investment, the amount is EXEMPT FROM SOCIAL SECURITY CONTRIBUTIONS but is TAXABLE in full, and is subject to CSG and CRDS at 9.70% ON 100% WITH NO 1.75% ALLOWANCE. Individual cap of 75% of the annual ceiling = EUR 36,045 for 2026 per scheme. NEODeS type '37 - Participation et interessement verses immediatement et directement par l'employeur'. The election is made within a statutory window; failing an election the amount is INVESTED BY DEFAULT. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: SUBJECT TO URSSAF GENERAL SCHEME is No while SUBJECT TO CSG / CRDS is Yes - the two are separate tests in France and this wage type is the clearest case of the difference. | S21.G00.54.001 / 37 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | No | Yes | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62211 | Statutory profit-sharing invested | Participation investie sur un plan d'epargne |  | Earning | Where the employee invests rather than takes cash, the amount is EXEMPT FROM INCOME TAX for the lock-in period as well as exempt from contributions, but remains subject to CSG and CRDS at 100% with no allowance. It is OUTSIDE GROSS PAY: it has its own regime and its own scheme administrator, and once invested it leaves the gross-to-net chain entirely - the scope manifest records it as out of scope for that reason. NEODeS type '11 - Participation y compris supplement'. Cap EUR 36,045 for 2026. | 62212 | Incentive scheme amount paid immediately | Interessement verse immediatement au salarie |  | Earning | An incentive scheme (interessement) is optional at any size, but employers of 11 to 49 employees meeting a sustained profitability test must operate AT LEAST ONE value-sharing device. | 62216 | Employer top-up to a company savings plan (PEE / PEI) | Abondement de l'employeur au PEE ou au PEI |  | Earning | Employer top-up to a company savings plan or an inter-company savings plan, within the caps set by reference to the annual ceiling. The EMPLOYEE'S own payment into the plan is a SAVING, not a contribution, and gives no deduction from the net taxable amount. NON-SUBSTITUTION PRINCIPLE: the exemption is lost if the payment substitutes for any element of remuneration paid by the employer, or which becomes compulsory under a rule, unless TWELVE MONTHS have elapsed between the withdrawal of that element and the first exempt payment. France has no general salary sacrifice mechanism and this is why. NON-SUBSTITUTION PRINCIPLE: the exemption is lost if the payment substitutes for any element of remuneration paid by the employer, or which becomes compulsory under a rule, unless TWELVE MONTHS have elapsed between the withdrawal of that element and the first exempt payment. | 62225 | Works council gift or voucher within the tolerance | Bon d'achat ou cadeau du CSE dans la limite de la tolerance |  | Earning | Gifts and vouchers within 5% of the monthly ceiling per event - EUR 200.25 for 2026 - are excluded from the contribution base, from CSG / CRDS and from income tax by administrative tolerance, provided the event is one of those listed in the doctrine and the benefit is awarded on a non-discriminatory basis. Carried in payroll for visibility only; it is NORMALLY MANAGED BY THE WORKS COUNCIL AND NOT BY PAYROLL AT ALL. EXEMPTION IS CONDITIONAL AND IS TESTED EVERY PERIOD. The exempt fraction is outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount; the excess above the limit is ordinary remuneration in full and enters every base from the first euro of excess. The limit tested is the one in force in the period TO WHICH the amount relates, not the period of payment. Where a condition of the regime fails, the whole amount - not only the excess - is requalified as remuneration and is regularised through a corrective DSN for the original period. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No | No | No | No |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. This wage type is an internal payroll line that is not carried under a code value of its own in NEODeS 2026.1; column G is therefore NA and no code is claimed. The instalment takes the regime (standard or enhanced) of the bonus it belongs to; where the enhanced conditions cease to be met between instalments, later instalments fall into the standard regime and earlier ones are not recomputed. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.52.001 / 046 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62302 | Annual leave pay adjustment | Regularisation de l'indemnite de conges payes |  | Earning | Correction of leave pay within the same calendar year, most often because the one-tenth base was incomplete when the comparison was first run. | 62305 | Compensatory leave indemnity on termination | Indemnite compensatrice de conges payes |  | Earning | ORDINARY REMUNERATION. This must be stated flatly because the line sits next to the severance payment in the same final settlement and is repeatedly given the severance treatment by mistake. Amount = max(one-tenth rule, salary maintenance rule) applied to the untaken days, covering both the current reference period AND any balance carried forward, including leave carried over after sickness. STILL PAYABLE AFTER DISMISSAL FOR SERIOUS MISCONDUCT - the old forfeiture was abolished and withholding it is unlawful. Leave continues to ACCRUE over a paid notice period the employee is released from working, and that accrual is added to the payout. Contributions due in full with the ceiling prorated to the employment period; CSG and CRDS on 98.25%, NOT on 100%, precisely because it is remuneration and not a rupture indemnity. | 62310 | Expatriation supplement - General Tax Code Article 81 A | Supplement d'expatriation - article 81 A du CGI |  | Earning | Supplement paid to an employee posted abroad by a French employer. EXEMPT FROM INCOME TAX WITHIN 40% of the remuneration the employee would have received for the same work in France, and only where the assignment abroad exceeds the statutory duration condition and the supplement is fixed in advance, separately from the salary, by reference to the number and duration of the stays. NEODeS type '900 - Indemnite d'expatriation', expressly a FISCAL-FIELD rubric. SOCIAL SECURITY IS DECIDED SEPARATELY AND ON A DIFFERENT TEST: an employee posted from France to another state continues to pay French contributions for the duration permitted by the applicable coordination instrument, while an employee working abroad outside any such instrument leaves the French compulsory scheme entirely. EXEMPTION IS CONDITIONAL AND IS TESTED EVERY PERIOD. The exempt fraction is outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount; the excess above the limit is ordinary remuneration in full and enters every base from the first euro of excess. The limit tested is the one in force in the period TO WHICH the amount relates, not the period of payment. Where a condition of the regime fails, the whole amount - not only the excess - is requalified as remuneration and is regularised through a corrective DSN for the original period. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: SUBJECT TO TAX is Yes-No because the supplement is exempt within 40% and taxable above it; the social columns are Yes-No because they turn on the coordination instrument, not on the tax exemption. | S21.G00.52.001 / 900 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62311 | Expatriation supplement arrears | Rappel de supplement d'expatriation |  | Earning | Expatriation supplement for a closed period. ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. The 40% income tax limit is tested against the remuneration OF THE PERIOD TO WHICH THE SUPPLEMENT RELATES. | 62320 | Impatriation premium - real amount | Prime d'impatriation - montant reel |  | Earning | Inbound-assignee regime, General Tax Code Article 155 B. The expatriation premium of an employee recruited abroad by a French company, or transferred within a group, is EXEMPT FROM INCOME TAX FOR ITS REAL AMOUNT where the employee was NOT tax-resident in France in the FIVE calendar years before taking up the post and becomes tax-resident in France. The exemption runs to 31 December of the EIGHTH calendar year following the year of taking up the post - a 2026 start therefore runs to 31 December 2034. Excluded from the net taxable amount MONTH BY MONTH. NEODeS type '901 - Indemnite d'impatriation', a fiscal-field rubric. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
