| 62360 | Home leave travel allowance | Indemnite de voyage de conge dans les foyers |  | Earning | Reimbursement of the assignee's periodic home leave travel. Where it is a genuine reimbursement of an evidenced outlay made necessary by the assignment it is a PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE and is outside every base; where it is a flat cash allowance with no evidence and no statutory limit behind it, it is remuneration in full. PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE within the Arrete of 20 December 2002. Outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount while it stays within the statutory limit and the outlay is genuinely incurred in the interest of the business. The excess above the limit is remuneration in full. Where the employer applies the sector-specific flat deduction (deduction forfaitaire specifique), the reimbursement is REINTEGRATED into the base before the percentage is applied - see FR-045. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: Every column is Yes-No because the answer turns entirely on whether the payment is evidenced as an expense within the Arrete of 20 December 2002 or is a flat allowance - a per-case determination payroll cannot pre-decide. | S21.G00.54.001 / 09 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62400 | Accommodation benefit in kind - flat valuation | Avantage en nature logement - evaluation forfaitaire |  | Earning | 2026 flat scale, eight bands by the employee's gross monthly pay expressed in multiples of the monthly social security ceiling: from EUR 79.70 per month for one room where gross monthly pay is below half the monthly ceiling (EUR 2,002.50), up to EUR 225.60 per room where gross monthly pay reaches one and a half ceilings (EUR 6,007.50). The bracket boundaries are pure multiples of the ceiling, which is how the scale revalues automatically each year - do NOT hard-code the boundaries as euro constants. The flat value INCLUDES water, gas, electricity, heating and a garage. BENEFIT IN KIND under Social Security Code Article L242-1 and the Arrete of 10 December 2002 as amended on 25 February 2025. France has NO fringe benefits tax: the value is added to gross for the calculation of every employee and employer contribution, of CSG / CRDS and of the net taxable amount, and the same value is then deducted after the contributions, so that net pay falls only by the contributions and the tax on the benefit. Any amount the employee actually pays toward the benefit is deducted from its value first. | S21.G00.54.001 / 03 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62420 | Housing allowance in cash | Indemnite de logement versee en especes |  | Earning | A CASH allowance toward the employee's housing. IT IS REMUNERATION IN FULL, not a benefit in kind and not a professional expense, because the employer provides no accommodation and the employee incurs no outlay in the interest of the business. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.52.001 / 048 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62440 | Temporary accommodation on a long-distance assignment | Frais d'hebergement en grand deplacement |  | Earning | Reimbursement of accommodation while the employee is prevented from returning home each day. A PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE within the flat limits of the Arrete of 20 December 2002, which set separate daily ceilings for Paris and the inner departments and for the rest of metropolitan France, and reduce them after 3 months and again after 24 months of assignment on the same site - a duration test that must be tracked per assignment, not per employee. PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE within the Arrete of 20 December 2002. Outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount while it stays within the statutory limit and the outlay is genuinely incurred in the interest of the business. The excess above the limit is remuneration in full. Where the employer applies the sector-specific flat deduction (deduction forfaitaire specifique), the reimbursement is REINTEGRATED into the base before the percentage is applied - see FR-045. | S21.G00.54.001 / 07 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62450 | Housing loan interest subsidy | Prise en charge d'interets d'emprunt immobilier |  | Earning | Employer payment of, or subsidy toward, the interest on the employee's housing loan, and any advantage arising from a loan granted by the employer at below the market rate. The ADVANTAGE - the interest saved - is a benefit in kind where it is material, and is remuneration in full. BENEFIT IN KIND under Social Security Code Article L242-1 and the Arrete of 10 December 2002 as amended on 25 February 2025. France has NO fringe benefits tax: the value is added to gross for the calculation of every employee and employer contribution, of CSG / CRDS and of the net taxable amount, and the same value is then deducted after the contributions, so that net pay falls only by the contributions and the tax on the benefit. Any amount the employee actually pays toward the benefit is deducted from its value first. | S21.G00.54.001 / 06 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62460 | Employer housing scheme individual assistance | Aide individuelle au logement financee par la contribution employeur |  | Earning | Individual assistance delivered to the employee out of the employer's housing obligations. THE EMPLOYER'S CONTRIBUTION ITSELF - the national housing aid fund levy at 0.10% on the capped band below 50 employees and 0.50% on total pay at 50 or more, and the 0.45% construction investment contribution above 50 employees - is an EMPLOYER TAX and never appears in the employee's gross-to-net chain. Where an individual advantage is actually delivered to the employee it is a benefit in kind unless it falls within a specific exemption. BENEFIT IN KIND under Social Security Code Article L242-1 and the Arrete of 10 December 2002 as amended on 25 February 2025. France has NO fringe benefits tax: the value is added to gross for the calculation of every employee and employer contribution, of CSG / CRDS and of the net taxable amount, and the same value is then deducted after the contributions, so that net pay falls only by the contributions and the tax on the benefit. Any amount the employee actually pays toward the benefit is deducted from its value first. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: Every column is Yes-No because the assistance may be delivered through the collecting body, in which case nothing enters the payslip, or directly by the employer, in which case it is a benefit in kind in full. | S21.G00.54.001 / 06 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62500 | Company vehicle benefit in kind | Avantage en nature vehicule |  | Earning | THE TREATMENT DEPENDS ON WHEN THE VEHICLE WAS MADE AVAILABLE, not on the year of the payslip, and BOTH SETS OF RULES ARE LIVE IN 2026. Made available BEFORE 1 February 2025: purchased - 9% of the inclusive purchase price if under 5 years old, 6% if older, rising to 12% and 9% where the employer pays for private fuel; leased - 30% of the total annual cost, capped at the value the purchase rules would give, or 40% with private fuel. Made available ON OR AFTER 1 February 2025: purchased - 15% and 10%, rising to 20% and 15% with private fuel; leased - 50% of the total annual cost, or 67% with private fuel. A vehicle TRANSFERRED to a different employee, or RENEWED, is a NEW availability and falls under the new rules. FULLY ELECTRIC VEHICLES: where the flat method is used the benefit is reduced by a 70% abatement capped at EUR 4,641.60 per year for 2026 (EUR 4,582 for 2025); only wholly electric vehicles qualify, hybrids do not; electricity supplied by the employer for charging is disregarded and a home charging point is valued at nil within the conditions of the official doctrine. BENEFIT IN KIND under Social Security Code Article L242-1 and the Arrete of 10 December 2002 as amended on 25 February 2025. France has NO fringe benefits tax: the value is added to gross for the calculation of every employee and employer contribution, of CSG / CRDS and of the net taxable amount, and the same value is then deducted after the contributions, so that net pay falls only by the contributions and the tax on the benefit. Any amount the employee actually pays toward the benefit is deducted from its value first. The COMPANY VEHICLE TAX on the fleet (taxes annuelles sur l'affectation des vehicules a des fins economiques) is a separate CORPORATE tax assessed on emissions and seniority, filed with the tax authorities and NOT through payroll; it must be flagged to the client's finance function and never modelled here. | S21.G00.54.001 / 04 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62523 | Voluntary employer transport reimbursement above 50% | Prise en charge facultative au-dela de 50 % de l'abonnement |  | Earning | An employer may reimburse more than the compulsory 50%. The voluntary excess is exempt from contributions and from income tax only within the specific statutory limit applicable to it; beyond that it is remuneration in full. Because that limit has moved repeatedly in recent finance acts, IT MUST BE READ FROM THE CURRENT INSTRUMENT before configuration and not carried forward from a previous year. EXEMPTION IS CONDITIONAL AND IS TESTED EVERY PERIOD. The exempt fraction is outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount; the excess above the limit is ordinary remuneration in full and enters every base from the first euro of excess. The limit tested is the one in force in the period TO WHICH the amount relates, not the period of payment. Where a condition of the regime fails, the whole amount - not only the excess - is requalified as remuneration and is regularised through a corrective DSN for the original period. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). | 62599 | Exceptional health-crisis allowance | Prime exceptionnelle liee a une crise sanitaire |  | Earning | HISTORICAL CARRIER, RETAINED FOR YEAR-TO-DATE MIGRATION AND FOR RETROSPECTIVE CORRECTIONS ONLY. The COVID-era exceptional purchasing-power bonus regime has ENDED; NEODeS retains type '902' as a 'potentiel nouveau type de prime exceptionnelle de pouvoir d'achat' precisely because no live regime occupies it. NO EXEMPTION MAY BE CONFIGURED ON THIS WAGE TYPE FOR 2026: any exceptional purchasing-power payment made in 2026 is either a value-sharing bonus (wage types 62218 / 62219, with their own conditions) or ordinary remuneration. Configuring a COVID-era exemption here is a known cross-year error. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62610 | Employer contribution to the compulsory health cover | Cotisation patronale a la complementaire sante obligatoire |  | Earning | At least 50% of the premium, compulsory for every private-sector employer under Labour Code Article L911-7. The scheme must at minimum cover the statutory minimum basket of care: the patient co-payment on general and hospital medicine, the full daily hospital charge of EUR 20 per day, dental prosthetics at 125% of the social security tariff and a defined optical allowance. THE RECURRING CONFIGURATION ERROR IS TO TREAT THIS AS FULLY EXEMPT. It is exempt from social security contributions within the envelope, SUBJECT TO CSG AND CRDS AT 100% WITH NO 1.75% ALLOWANCE, and ADDED TO THE EMPLOYEE'S NET TAXABLE AMOUNT - non-deductible for income tax since 2014. Omitting the last of the three under-withholds every month. TWO DIFFERENT LIMITS APPLY TO THE SAME PREMIUM AND THIS IS A CLASSIC SOURCE OF ERROR. SOCIAL exemption, Social Security Code Article D242-1: health and death-and-disability cover TAKEN TOGETHER, 6% of the annual ceiling (EUR 2,883.60) plus 1.5% of the employee's gross pay, the total not exceeding 12% of the annual ceiling (EUR 5,767.20); supplementary pension cover separately, the higher of 5% of the annual ceiling (EUR 2,403.00) or 5% of pay capped at five annual ceilings. TAX deduction, General Tax Code Article 83: for death-and-disability cover, 5% of the annual ceiling plus 2% of remuneration, the total not exceeding 2% of eight annual ceilings; for supplementary pension cover, 8% of gross annual remuneration itself capped at eight annual ceilings. Amounts above the SOCIAL limits are reintegrated into the contribution base. Note the combined envelope: an employer whose health premium already absorbs it will find its death-and-disability premium partly reintegrated even though each policy looks compliant in isolation. THE SCHEME MUST BE COLLECTIVE AND COMPULSORY to be exempt at all: it must cover an objectively defined category of staff and membership must be compulsory for everyone in that category. A scheme offered on an individual opt-in basis LOSES THE EXEMPTION ENTIRELY and the employer's contribution becomes ordinary pay. Exemptions from membership are lawful only where the employee requests them in writing with evidence AND the founding document provides for them; an exemption granted without evidence is the most common finding in a contribution inspection of this area, and the employer's contribution is then treated as ordinary pay for the whole period concerned. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: SUBJECT TO URSSAF GENERAL SCHEME is Yes-No: exempt within the Article D242-1 envelope, reintegrated into the ordinary contribution base above it. | S21.G00.54.001 / 92 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes-No | Yes | No | Yes | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. Amounts above the SOCIAL limits are reintegrated into the contribution base. | 62630 | Meal allowance and working-clothes maintenance allowance | Prime de panier et indemnite d'entretien des vetements de travail |  | Earning | Flat allowances of the Arrete of 20 December 2002 covering the extra cost of a meal taken at the place of work under constrained conditions, and the maintenance of working clothes the employer requires. PROFESSIONAL EXPENSES outside every base within their flat annual limits, and remuneration in full above them. A dirt or risk premium (wage type 62155) is NOT the same thing and is remuneration whatever its label. PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE within the Arrete of 20 December 2002. | 62645 | Employer pension contribution above the exemption limit - reintegrated | Cotisation patronale de retraite supplementaire reintegree dans l'assiette |  | Earning | The excess of the employer's supplementary pension contribution above the Article D242-1 limit. REINTEGRATED INTO THE ORDINARY CONTRIBUTION BASE IN FULL, on top of the CSG / CRDS liability and the social lump-sum levy that already applied to the exempt part. The reintegration is computed on an ANNUAL basis and is normally regularised in December or in the final payslip, because the limit contains a percentage-of-pay component. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.54.001 / 93 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62660 | Homeworking allowance | Allocation forfaitaire de teletravail |  | Earning | Flat allowance covering the costs the employee bears in working from home. Exempt from contributions, from CSG / CRDS and from income tax within the flat daily, weekly and monthly limits set by the official social security doctrine, which scale with the number of days worked from home per week, OR at the real amount on evidence. Above the flat limits the excess is remuneration unless the real cost is evidenced. PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE within the Arrete of 20 December 2002. Outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount while it stays within the statutory limit and the outlay is genuinely incurred in the interest of the business. The excess above the limit is remuneration in full. Where the employer applies the sector-specific flat deduction (deduction forfaitaire specifique), the reimbursement is REINTEGRATED into the base before the percentage is applied - see FR-045. | 62661 | Homeworking allowance arrears | Rappel d'allocation de teletravail |  | Earning | Homeworking allowance for a closed period, tested against the limits of that period. ARREARS. France has NO separate schedule for a retrospective payment: no spreading, no annualisation, no exceptional-payment table. The amount is added to the net taxable amount of the month in which it is PAID and bears the withholding-at-source rate then in force (General Tax Code Article 204 A). Social security follows the PERIOD OF WORK instead: the amount is declared in a DSN carrying the attachment period, at the rates and the ceiling of that period, and the progressive ceiling reconciliation is re-run for it. PROFESSIONAL EXPENSE within the Arrete of 20 December 2002. Outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount while it stays within the statutory limit and the outlay is genuinely incurred in the interest of the business. | 62670 | Employer share of meal vouchers | Participation patronale aux titres-restaurant |  | Earning | EXEMPT UP TO EUR 7.32 PER VOUCHER FOR 2026, provided the employer's share is between 50% AND 60% of the face value - which fixes the compliant face-value range at EUR 12.20 to EUR 14.64 (7.32 / 0.60 and 7.32 / 0.50). One voucher per working day on which a meal falls within the working hours, and only for employees with no company canteen. Outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside income tax within the limit (Social Security Code Article L136-1-1 III and General Tax Code Article 81, 19). ANY EXCESS IS FULLY SUBJECT to contributions and to income tax. NEODeS type '17'. The employee share is wage type 21670. EXEMPTION IS CONDITIONAL AND IS TESTED EVERY PERIOD. The exempt fraction is outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount; the excess above the limit is ordinary remuneration in full and enters every base from the first euro of excess. The limit tested is the one in force in the period TO WHICH the amount relates, not the period of payment. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: As wage type 62750. | S21.G00.54.001 / 06 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62873 | Employer sick pay top-up | Maintien de salaire employeur en cas de maladie |  | Earning | Labour Code Article L1226-1 and Article D1226-1. Available after ONE YEAR'S SERVICE, after a SEVEN-DAY WAITING PERIOD for non-occupational sickness and with NO waiting period for a work accident or occupational disease, at 90% OF THE GROSS PAY THE EMPLOYEE WOULD HAVE RECEIVED FOR THE FIRST 30 DAYS and 66.66% FOR THE FOLLOWING 30, each period increased by 10 days per five years of service beyond the first, up to 90 days at each rate. The top-up is computed as (90% or 66.66% x the pay the employee would have received) LESS the daily social security benefit already counted. Collective agreements very frequently improve on this - full pay from day one is common for managerial grades - and the more favourable rule applies. THE TOP-UP IS ORDINARY PAY IN EVERY RESPECT; the exemption attaching to the statutory benefit does not extend to it. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62875 | Daily sickness benefit under subrogation | Indemnites journalieres de securite sociale - maladie, avec subrogation |  | Earning | 50% of the daily base wage, itself the average gross of the last three months divided by 91.25, the base being capped at 1.4 TIMES THE MINIMUM WAGE - a cap reduced from 1.8 by Decret n. 2025-160 of 20 February 2025. BECAUSE THE MINIMUM WAGE ROSE ON 1 JUNE 2026 THE CAP MOVED DURING THE YEAR: maximum gross daily benefit EUR 41.95 from 1 February 2026 to 30 June 2026, and EUR 42.97 from 1 July 2026. A THREE-DAY WAITING PERIOD applies before the benefit begins. DAILY SOCIAL SECURITY BENEFIT TRANSITING THE PAYSLIP UNDER SUBROGATION. Subrogation changes the payslip structure: where the employer is subrogated it maintains pay, shows the benefit as an offsetting line and receives the benefit from the sickness insurance fund; where it is not, the fund pays the employee directly and the payslip shows only the deduction and the top-up. Both are lawful, the collective agreement usually decides, and the choice must be recorded per client AND PER ABSENCE TYPE. The benefit is EXEMPT FROM SOCIAL SECURITY CONTRIBUTIONS but SUBJECT TO CSG AT THE REDUCED 6.20% RATE and to CRDS at 0.50%, and it is FULLY TAXABLE - ordinary sickness benefits are not exempt, contrary to a common assumption. | S21.G00.54.001 / 06 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | No | Yes | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62881 | Settlement sum compensating loss of employment | Indemnite transactionnelle reparant la perte d'emploi |  | Earning | A SETTLEMENT SUM IS NOT AUTOMATICALLY SEVERANCE. The collecting body looks at WHAT EACH ELEMENT ACTUALLY COMPENSATES, and a settlement that is silent on its composition risks being reclassified as ordinary pay IN FULL. Where the sum genuinely compensates the loss of employment it follows the severance regime. NEODeS type '022 - Indemnite transactionnelle'; type '055 - Indemnite transactionnelle exoneree fiscalement' exists for the income-tax-exempt fraction. The 40% employer contribution applies ONLY where the underlying termination was a mutually agreed termination or a compulsory retirement. THREE SEPARATE EXEMPTION TESTS APPLY TO THE SAME AMOUNT AND GIVE THREE DIFFERENT ANSWERS - do not compute one and reuse it. (1) Income tax, General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies: exempt up to the HIGHEST of the legal or collectively agreed amount (no cap), twice the previous calendar year's gross, or half the total indemnity - the last two capped at 6 PASS = EUR 288,360. (2) Social security contributions, Article L242-1 III: exempt for the income-tax-exempt fraction, itself capped at 2 PASS = EUR 96,120. (3) CSG / CRDS, Article L136-1-1: exempt only up to the LOWER of the legal or agreed amount and the contribution-exempt fraction; the excess bears 9.70% on 100% of the amount with NO 1.75% allowance, and that levy is entirely non-deductible for income tax (Article 154 quinquies). CLIFF: where the AGGREGATE of all indemnities paid on the termination exceeds 10 PASS = EUR 480,600, every social exemption is lost and the whole package is contributory and CSG / CRDS-liable FROM THE FIRST EURO. Test the aggregate before any of the three tests above. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: Every column is Yes-No because the treatment depends on what the settlement compensates and on the three separate exemption tests with three different ceilings. | S21.G00.52.001 / 022 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

None of the severance exemptions applies. This is the outcome a silent settlement risks, and it is why the composition should always be stated in the agreement. ORDINARY REMUNERATION under Social Security Code Article L242-1: in the contribution base in full and in every band - capped band to EUR 4,005 per month (old-age 6.90% employee / 8.55% employer), uncapped base (health 13.00% employer, old-age 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances 5.25%, autonomy solidarity 0.30%, the establishment's industrial-accident net rate, social dialogue 0.016% each side, the mobility levy where a transport authority has set a rate), Agirc-Arrco band 1 to EUR 4,005 (3.15% / 4.72%) and band 2 from EUR 4,005 to EUR 32,040 (8.64% / 12.95%), the general balance contribution (0.86% / 1.29% band 1; 1.08% / 1.62% band 2), the technical balance contribution (0.14% / 0.21%) for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling, the executive employment association contribution (0.024% / 0.036%, managerial grade), and unemployment insurance 4.00% plus the wage guarantee scheme 0.25% to four ceilings (EUR 16,020 per month). CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.52.001 / 022 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62883 | Court damages for dismissal without real and serious cause | Dommages et interets pour licenciement sans cause reelle et serieuse |  | Earning | Awarded by the labour court under Labour Code Article L1235-3, within the statutory scale, which uses a lower band for the first ten years of service where the employer has fewer than 11 employees. FULLY EXEMPT FROM INCOME TAX (General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies, 1). Exempt from social security contributions to 2 PASS = EUR 96,120, and from CSG / CRDS only to the legal or collectively agreed redundancy amount. THREE SEPARATE EXEMPTION TESTS APPLY TO THE SAME AMOUNT AND GIVE THREE DIFFERENT ANSWERS - do not compute one and reuse it. (1) Income tax, General Tax Code Article 80 duodecies: exempt up to the HIGHEST of the legal or collectively agreed amount (no cap), twice the previous calendar year's gross, or half the total indemnity - the last two capped at 6 PASS = EUR 288,360. (2) Social security contributions, Article L242-1 III: exempt for the income-tax-exempt fraction, itself capped at 2 PASS = EUR 96,120. (3) CSG / CRDS, Article L136-1-1: exempt only up to the LOWER of the legal or agreed amount and the contribution-exempt fraction; the excess bears 9.70% on 100% of the amount with NO 1.75% allowance, and that levy is entirely non-deductible for income tax (Article 154 quinquies). CLIFF: where the AGGREGATE of all indemnities paid on the termination exceeds 10 PASS = EUR 480,600, every social exemption is lost and the whole package is contributory and CSG / CRDS-liable FROM THE FIRST EURO. Test the aggregate before any of the three tests above. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: SUBJECT TO TAX is No - the income tax exemption is unlimited - but the social columns remain Yes-No because the 2 PASS ceiling and the CSG limit still bite. | S21.G00.52.001 / 017 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| 62890 | Free share award - employer reporting | Attribution gratuite d'actions - declaration employeur |  | Notional | REPORTING CARRIER ONLY - no payment, no withholding, no base. The acquisition gain from a free share award is taxable in the year of DISPOSAL, OUTSIDE withholding at source and outside the ordinary contribution bands; the employee pays through the annual assessment. The employer declares the award and pays the specific 30% employer contribution (wage type 62993); the employee's 10% specific contribution above EUR 300,000 is wage type 21890. The scope manifest records free share and share option gains as OUT OF SCOPE for the gross-to-net chain for exactly this reason - only the two specific contributions touch the DSN. | NA |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No | No | No | No |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. This wage type is an internal payroll line that is not carried under a code value of its own in NEODeS 2026.1; column G is therefore NA and no code is claimed. Checked against the code lists of S21.G00.51.011, S21.G00.52.001, S21.G00.54.001, S21.G00.58.003, S21.G00.78.001 and S21.G00.81.001 in FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/ct2026_rubriques.json. |
| 62900 | Internship gratuity | Gratification de stage |  | Earning | EXEMPT FROM SOCIAL SECURITY CONTRIBUTIONS up to 15% OF THE HOURLY SOCIAL SECURITY CEILING PER HOUR OF ATTENDANCE - EUR 4.50 PER HOUR FOR 2026, the hourly ceiling being EUR 30. ONLY THE EXCESS above that threshold is a contribution base. Exempt from income tax up to the annual minimum wage per calendar year (General Tax Code Article 81 bis). An intern is NOT an employee: they receive a gratuity, not a wage, and are outside the minimum wage entirely. Requires a tripartite internship agreement, and the exemption carries its own contribution code '022'. EXEMPTION IS CONDITIONAL AND IS TESTED EVERY PERIOD. The exempt fraction is outside the contribution base, outside CSG / CRDS and outside the net taxable amount; the excess above the limit is ordinary remuneration in full and enters every base from the first euro of excess. The limit tested is the one in force in the period TO WHICH the amount relates, not the period of payment. Where a condition of the regime fails, the whole amount - not only the excess - is requalified as remuneration and is regularised through a corrective DSN for the original period. YES-NO JUSTIFICATION: Every column is Yes-No because the gratuity is exempt to EUR 4.50 per hour of attendance for contributions and to the annual minimum wage for income tax, and is fully liable above each. | S21.G00.51.011 / 001 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes-No | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.51.011 (Type d'element de remuneration) and S21.G00.51.013 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62960 | Compensatory notice indemnity | Indemnite compensatrice de preavis |  | Earning | Paid where the employer releases the employee from working the notice period, Labour Code Article L1234-5. The only alternative is a transfer into a retirement savings plan within the statutory limits (wage type 62646), which must be elected before the payout. CSG 9.20% and CRDS 0.50% on 98.25% of the amount up to four annual ceilings (EUR 192,240), then on 100%. Included in the net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) and therefore in the withholding-at-source base. | S21.G00.52.001 / 025 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.52.001 (Type de prime, gratification et indemnite) and S21.G00.52.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| 62980 | Final settlement balancing line | Solde de tout compte - ligne d'ajustement |  | Earning | Residual balancing line of the final settlement (solde de tout compte). THE FINAL SETTLEMENT IS NOT ONE TAXABLE LUMP: it is made up of items with different treatments, two of which - the compensatory notice indemnity and the compensatory leave indemnity - are ordinary pay in every respect while the others carry the three-layer exemption regime. Treating the settlement as a single lump is the single most damaging modelling error in this area. THE CEILING RECONCILIATION AND THE ANNUAL RECONCILIATION OF THE UNIFIED DEGRESSIVE REDUCTION MUST BOTH BE RUN IN THE FINAL PAYSLIP, not deferred to the year end. | FR-001 | General social contribution - deductible portion (CSG deductible) | Contribution sociale generalisee deductible |  | Deduction | 6.80% of the CSG / CRDS base. THE BASE IS NOT GROSS PAY: it is gross pay reduced by a 1.75% professional-expense allowance on the portion up to FOUR ANNUAL CEILINGS (EUR 192,240 for 2026), with NO allowance above that, PLUS the employer's contributions to supplementary health, death-and-disability and supplementary pension cover taken at 100% WITH NO ALLOWANCE (Social Security Code Article L136-1-1). DEDUCTIBLE FROM THE NET TAXABLE AMOUNT. The 1.75% allowance does NOT apply to rupture indemnities, to replacement incomes or to the employer cover contributions. YEAR-END ADJUSTMENT REQUIRED: once cumulative pay passes four annual ceilings the allowance ceases and any excess already granted is recovered. | S21.G00.81.001 / 072 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

NOT DEDUCTIBLE from the net taxable amount - it is ADDED BACK when the net taxable amount is built, which is why the net taxable amount is HIGHER than the net payable before tax. A system that applies the withholding rate to net pay under-withholds by roughly the value of this contribution, the CRDS and the employer health contribution. Together with FR-001 the CSG totals 9.20%. | S21.G00.81.001 / 072 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

ENTIRELY NON-DEDUCTIBLE from the net taxable amount and added back with the non-deductible CSG. Ordinance n. 96-50 of 24 January 1996. CSG and CRDS together come to 9.70% and are the employee's health and solidarity funding - THEY ARE NOT INCOME TAX: they are governed by the Social Security Code, collected by URSSAF, calculated on a different base, and must never be modelled as a withholding. | S21.G00.81.001 / 079 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-004 | Alsace-Moselle additional local health contribution | Cotisation supplementaire maladie du regime local d'Alsace-Moselle |  | Deduction | 1.30% employee contribution on TOTAL PAY, maintained at that rate for 2026 by the local scheme's board, in Bas-Rhin, Haut-Rhin and Moselle. TRIGGERED BY THE PLACE OF WORK, NOT BY THE EMPLOYEE'S RESIDENCE - an employee living in Strasbourg but working in Nancy does not pay it, and an employee living in Nancy but working in Strasbourg does. It is the ONLY employee health contribution in France. Alsace-Moselle also has two additional public holidays (Good Friday and 26 December) and a reduced apprenticeship tax rate of 0.44%, all on the same place-of-work test. Deductible from the net taxable amount. | S21.G00.81.001 / 115 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-005 | Withholding at source (prelevement a la source) | Prelevement a la source de l'impot sur le revenu |  | Tax | Withholding = NET TAXABLE AMOUNT FOR THE PERIOD x THE APPLICABLE RATE, rounded to the nearest euro for the remittance with 0.50 counting as 1.00. THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE EMPLOYER'S ARITHMETIC. The employer performs NO tax computation: the tax administration calculates the rate from the employee's most recent annual return, taking the whole tax household into account, and transmits it through the DSN return flow. The employer never sees the family situation, never applies an allowance and NEVER OPERATES A PROGRESSIVE SCALE - configuring the income tax scale into a payroll engine is a category error, and the separate default grid exists to prevent it. FRANCE DOES NOT ANNUALISE: no projection, no estimate of full-year income, no cumulative recalculation, and NO EMPLOYER YEAR-END RECONCILIATION of income tax at all. | S21.G00.50.009 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.50.009 (Montant de l'impot sur le revenu preleve a la source) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-006 | Withholding-at-source rate transmitted by the tax administration | Taux de prelevement a la source transmis par l'administration fiscale |  | Percentage | The personal rate, an individualised rate for a couple, or a rate the employee has had reduced or increased. THE EMPLOYER APPLIES IT AS RECEIVED AND ASKS NO QUESTIONS. A rate of 0% must be applied exactly as transmitted; the employer may not substitute a positive rate. TRANSMITTED RATES CARRY A VALIDITY PERIOD: on expiry, if no new rate has arrived, the employer REVERTS TO THE DEFAULT GRID. A payroll engine must DATE-CHECK THE RATE ON EVERY RUN rather than treating it as a static employee attribute. A rate arriving mid-month applies from the NEXT payslip, not retrospectively. The rate type is declared in S21.G00.50.007 and the rate identifier in .008. | S21.G00.50.006 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.50.006 (Taux de prelevement a la source), .007 (Type du taux), .008 (Identifiant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-007 | Default withholding grid (grille de taux par defaut) | Grille de taux par defaut du prelevement a la source |  | Percentage | Applied where no rate has been transmitted - a new hire, a first-time taxpayer, an employee who has elected confidentiality. IT IS NOT A PENALTY RATE: it is the statutory fallback and it is often close to, and can be lower than, the personal rate. It is a FLAT RATE READ FROM A BAND, not a progressive scale - the whole amount is taxed at the band's rate with no slicing. TWO METROPOLITAN GRIDS ARE LIVE IN 2026 and both must be held: the 2025 grid for income PAID from 1 January to 30 April 2026 (0% below EUR 1,620, rising through twenty bands to 43% from EUR 55,062), and the 2026 grid for income PAID from 1 May 2026 (0% below EUR 1,635, rising to 43% from EUR 55,558), the 2026 grid being the 2025 grid revalued by 0.9%. THE APPLICABLE GRID IS DECIDED BY THE DATE THE INCOME IS PAID: a payment made on 30 April uses the first grid and one made on 4 May uses the second, and no retrospective adjustment is made for January to April. Separate grids apply for Guadeloupe, Martinique and La Reunion (thresholds from EUR 1,875, top band from EUR 60,893) and for Guyane and Mayotte (from EUR 2,008, top band from EUR 64,341). THE GRID IS FREQUENCY-SENSITIVE: monthly, quarterly, weekly, daily and hourly versions are published and the version matching the pay period must be used - applying the monthly grid to a weekly payment produces a 0% rate on nearly every payslip. | S21.G00.50.007 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.50.007 (Type du taux de prelevement a la source) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-008 | Short-contract allowance on the default grid | Abattement pour contrat court applique a la grille par defaut |  | Intermediary | Where the employee is on a fixed-term contract or an assignment whose INITIAL TERM does not exceed TWO MONTHS and no rate has been transmitted, the default grid is applied AFTER deducting an allowance of EUR 748 PER MONTH, or EUR 766 FROM 1 JUNE 2026 - the January figure may be kept for the whole year by administrative tolerance. Prorated for a part month. The allowance reduces the amount against which the grid band is read AND the amount the rate is applied to. It applies ONLY on the default grid: once a personal rate arrives, the allowance stops. | S21.G00.50.012 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.50.012 (Montant de l'abattement sur la base fiscale) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-009 | Non-resident specific withholding - General Tax Code Article 182 A | Retenue a la source sur les salaires des non-residents - article 182 A du CGI |  | Tax | AN ENTIRELY SEPARATE MECHANISM FROM WITHHOLDING AT SOURCE - different base, different rates, different legal text, different DSN reporting. NOTHING MAY BE READ ACROSS FROM ONE TO THE OTHER. Base: net taxable amount AFTER A 10% FLAT PROFESSIONAL-EXPENSE DEDUCTION. Method: PROGRESSIVE SLICING across the bands of the PERIOD - monthly for 2026: 0% up to EUR 1,440, 12% from EUR 1,441 to EUR 4,176, 20% above EUR 4,176; annually EUR 17,275 and EUR 50,112; quarterly EUR 4,319 and EUR 12,528; weekly EUR 332 and EUR 964; daily EUR 55 and EUR 161. THE 0% AND 12% FRACTIONS ARE LIBERATORY - the tax is final and the income is excluded from the annual assessment; only the 20% fraction is creditable and triggers a return. In the overseas departments the 12% and 20% rates fall to 8% and 14.4%. A treaty may remove France's right to tax altogether, in which case no withholding is due; treaty analysis is a per-assignment determination and is not automatable. Note that SOCIAL SECURITY IS DECIDED INDEPENDENTLY: a non-resident for tax may be fully liable to French social security if they work in France. | S21.G00.81.001 / 077 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-010 | Withholding-at-source correction for a closed period | Regularisation du prelevement a la source |  | Tax | Where the employer discovers that it withheld the wrong amount for a past period - a wrong rate, a wrong base - the correction is made through the DEDICATED DSN CORRECTION BLOCK for the period concerned (S21.G00.56, carrying the month of the error, the error type, the corrected net taxable amount and the corrected rate), NOT by adjusting the current month. Within the same calendar year the correction flows through to the employee's net pay; after the year end the employer files a corrective DSN and the employee obtains the correction through the annual assessment - THE EMPLOYER DOES NOT REPAY THE EMPLOYEE DIRECTLY FOR A CLOSED YEAR. An employee who considers the rate too high does not seek a refund from the employer at all: they ask the tax administration to reduce the rate, and the employer plays no part. | S21.G00.56.001 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.56.001 to .015 (Regularisation de prelevement a la source) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-011 | Net taxable amount (remuneration nette fiscale) | Remuneration nette fiscale |  | Base | Gross pay for the period, LESS employee old-age contributions capped and uncapped, LESS the Agirc-Arrco, general balance and technical balance contributions, LESS the deductible CSG at 6.80%, LESS the employee share of the compulsory health cover and of a death-and-disability or supplementary pension scheme within the Article 83 limits; PLUS the non-deductible CSG at 2.40%, PLUS the CRDS at 0.50%, PLUS the employer contribution to the compulsory health cover, PLUS the employer contribution to a death-and-disability scheme; LESS exempt overtime within the EUR 7,500 annual cap, LESS the exempt fraction of any termination payment, LESS the exempt fraction of an expatriation or impatriation premium. TWO DERIVATIONS ARE LOAD-BEARING AND ARE WRONG IN A SURPRISING NUMBER OF IMPLEMENTATIONS: the employer's health-cover contribution IS ADDED, being non-deductible for income tax since 2014, and omitting it under-withholds every month; and THE NET TAXABLE AMOUNT IS HIGHER THAN THE NET PAYABLE BEFORE TAX - it is not a variant of the net figure. Not in it at all: reimbursed professional expenses within the limits, meal-voucher employer shares within the limits, the 50% transport reimbursement, and invested profit-sharing and incentive-scheme amounts. | S21.G00.50.002 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.50.002 (Remuneration nette fiscale) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-012 | Employer health, maternity, disability and death contribution | Cotisation patronale d'assurance maladie, maternite, invalidite, deces |  | Employer Contribution | 13.00% ON THE WHOLE OF GROSS PAY WITH NO CEILING. THE SINGLE MOST IMPORTANT EMPLOYER CHANGE FOR 2026: the REDUCED RATE OF 7.00% for employees paid below a defined multiple of the minimum wage IS ABOLISHED from 1 January 2026. Every employer now applies the full 13.00%, and the value of the former reduced band is delivered instead through the unified degressive general reduction of FR-024 as a separate calculated deduction. An engine carrying a 2025 reduced-rate band will under-charge every low-paid employee. | S21.G00.81.001 / 075 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| FR-024 | Unified degressive general reduction of employer contributions | Reduction generale degressive unifiee des cotisations patronales |  | Employer Contribution | C = Tmin + Tdelta x [ 0.5 x ( 3 x annual reference minimum wage / annual gross pay - 1 ) ] ^ 1.75, with for 2026: Tmin = 0.0200; Tdelta = 0.3781 where the employer pays the housing fund at 0.10% and 0.3821 at 0.50%; exponent 1.75; maximum coefficient 0.3981 or 0.4021 respectively. ANNUAL REFERENCE MINIMUM WAGE = EUR 21,876.40 (EUR 12.02 x 1,820 hours), FROZEN FOR THE WHOLE OF 2026 by Decret n. 2026-509 of 12 June 2026 - the 1 June minimum wage increase is NOT passed through, so from 1 June an employer pays a higher wage but calculates its relief against the January reference. A PAYROLL ENGINE THAT READS A SINGLE 'CURRENT MINIMUM WAGE' VARIABLE WILL SILENTLY OVERSTATE THE RELIEF FOR SEVEN MONTHS OF THE YEAR. The coefficient is rounded to four decimals. Eligibility: annual gross pay BELOW 3 x EUR 21,876.40 = EUR 65,629.20. BEHAVIOUR AT THE THRESHOLD IS A CLIFF OF TWO PERCENTAGE POINTS, NOT A TAPER: the coefficient falls to its floor of 0.0200 as pay approaches the threshold and the reduction then stops altogether - an employee on EUR 65,629 attracts relief of about 2% of pay and one on EUR 65,630 attracts none. An engine modelling a smooth decay to zero understates the relief across the whole upper range and misses the cliff. Pay taken into account is annual gross pay subject to contributions INCREASED BY ANY VALUE-SHARING BONUS PAID IN THE YEAR, which is outside the contribution bands but inside this reference pay. The calculation is ANNUAL, applied provisionally each month on year-to-date figures and reconciled cumulatively, definitively in December OR IN THE FINAL PAYSLIP. The reduction is imputed against the employer contributions it covers: health, capped and uncapped old-age, family allowances, the national housing aid fund, the autonomy solidarity contribution, the Agirc-Arrco and general balance contributions, unemployment insurance, the social dialogue contribution, and a FIXED 0.49 PERCENTAGE POINTS of the industrial-accident contribution - the mutualised fraction set for 2026 by Social Security Code Article D241-2-4, down from 0.50 in 2025. The establishment's ACTUAL industrial-accident rate is NOT used in the reduction; only the 0.49 points. | S21.G00.81.001 / 018 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | No | Yes | No | Yes |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.81.001 (Code de cotisation individuelle) and S21.G00.81.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

An employer cost. THE EMPLOYEE'S DISABLED-WORKER RECOGNITION IS DECLARED ONLY IF THE EMPLOYEE CHOOSES TO DECLARE IT - the employer may not require it, and the certificate drives the headcount for this obligation, not any payroll calculation. | S21.G00.40.072 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | Yes | No | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.40.072 and the DOETH blocks of the DSN, per FR-URSSAF-003-DOETH-Spec.xlsx - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-040 | Monthly social security ceiling | Plafond mensuel de la securite sociale |  | Base | EUR 4,005 per month and EUR 48,060 per year for 2026 (Arrete of 22 December 2025). The annual ceiling is EXACTLY twelve times the monthly ceiling. Other periodicities: quarterly EUR 12,015, daily EUR 220, hourly EUR 30 for engagements shorter than five hours. THE CEILING MUST BE TAKEN FROM THE DECREE AND NEVER COMPUTED FROM THE ANNOUNCED PERCENTAGE: the monthly ceiling rose from EUR 3,925 in 2025, a ratio of 1.02038 and not the announced '+2%', because the decree applies forecast average-wage growth PLUS a correction of the previous year's estimate, and then rounds. The ceiling derives from the AVERAGE WAGE PER HEAD in the non-agricultural market sector (Article D242-17), a different wage aggregate from the minimum wage - THREE DIFFERENT WAGE REFERENCES ARE SIMULTANEOUSLY LIVE IN 2026 and a single 'minimum wage' parameter cannot express them. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. DSN contribution base code '02 - Assiette brute plafonnee'. | FR-042 | Uncapped contribution base (assiette deplafonnee) | Assiette brute deplafonnee |  | Base | Total gross pay with no upper limit - all sums paid in consideration of or on the occasion of the work (Social Security Code Article L242-1). Carries health at 13.00%, uncapped old-age at 0.40% / 2.11%, family allowances at 5.25%, industrial accidents, autonomy solidarity at 0.30%, social dialogue, the mobility levy and the training and apprenticeship levies. DSN base code '03'. Carries Agirc-Arrco at 3.15% / 4.72% and the general balance contribution at 0.86% / 1.29%. IT IS NOT THE SAME AS THE CAPPED BAND OF FR-041 even though the two share a boundary in 2026: they belong to different schemes, are declared separately and can diverge - using one set of bands for both is a defect. | S21.G00.78.001 / 02 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.78.001 (Code de base assujettie) and S21.G00.78.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

ABOVE EIGHT CEILINGS NO SUPPLEMENTARY PENSION CONTRIBUTION IS DUE at all; the uncapped basic contributions and the social levies continue. The technical balance contribution runs on bands 1 and 2 together but ONLY for employees whose pay exceeds one ceiling - a threshold test on the employee, not a band. | S21.G00.78.001 / 22 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.78.001 (Code de base assujettie) and S21.G00.78.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-045 | Sector-specific flat deduction (deduction forfaitaire specifique) | Deduction forfaitaire specifique pour frais professionnels |  | Intermediary | A percentage reduction of the SOCIAL SECURITY CONTRIBUTION BASE ONLY, for the occupations listed in Article 5 of the annex to the Decret of 17 November 1936 - construction, journalism, commercial travellers, performing artists and others. Method: gross pay is FIRST INCREASED by any reimbursed professional expenses, THEN reduced by the percentage; the result is the contribution base. CAPPED AT EUR 7,600 PER EMPLOYEE PER YEAR. The percentage is being progressively reduced by ONE POINT A YEAR TOWARD ABOLITION - for journalists it stands at 24% in 2026, so the rate is a dated parameter and must not be carried forward. IT DOES NOT REDUCE THE INCOME TAX BASE. Requires a company or sector agreement authorising it AND the employee's consent, renewed annually. DSN type '10 - Deduction forfaitaire specifique', with the reintegrated expenses declared separately under type '35'. | S21.G00.54.001 / 10 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | Yes | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.54.001 (Autre element de revenu brut) and S21.G00.54.002 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |
| FR-046 | CSG and CRDS base | Assiette de la contribution sociale generalisee |  | Base | Gross pay x 98.25% on the portion up to FOUR ANNUAL CEILINGS (EUR 192,240 for 2026), then x 100%; PLUS the employer's contributions to supplementary health, death-and-disability and supplementary pension cover taken at 100% WITH NO ALLOWANCE. DSN base code '04'. THE 1.75% ALLOWANCE DOES NOT APPLY to rupture indemnities, to replacement incomes or to the employer cover contributions, and it CEASES ENTIRELY once cumulative pay passes four annual ceilings - any excess already granted must then be recovered, which is one of the four compulsory year-end adjustments. THIS BASE NEVER COINCIDES WITH THE CONTRIBUTION BASE OF FR-042, and treating 'subject to contributions' and 'subject to CSG' as one test is the most common structural error in a French implementation. | S21.G00.78.001 / 04 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.78.001 (Code de base assujettie) and S21.G00.78.004 (Montant) - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

| FR-048 | Progressive ceiling reconciliation | Regularisation progressive du plafond de la securite sociale |  | Intermediary | Compares CUMULATIVE pay to the CUMULATIVE ceiling and corrects the split between the capped and uncapped bands. Run EVERY MONTH and definitively in December OR IN THE FINAL PAYSLIP - never deferred to the year end for a leaver. SINCE JANUARY 2022 THE CEILING IS NO LONGER PRORATED CONTRACT BY CONTRACT: the reconciliation takes account of all sums paid since the start date of the FIRST contract in the year, so a temporary employee returning for a second assignment continues the same cumulative calculation rather than restarting it. One of the four compulsory year-end adjustments, alongside the annual reconciliation of the unified degressive reduction, the capping of the 1.75% CSG allowance and the reconciliation of exempt overtime against the EUR 7,500 cap. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. | FR-052 | Wage attachment - seizable fraction | Saisie sur remuneration - quotite saisissable |  | Deduction | THE PROCEDURE WAS FUNDAMENTALLY REFORMED WITH EFFECT FROM 1 JULY 2025 (Act n. 2023-1059 of 20 November 2023 and the Decret of 12 February 2025): it is no longer judicial. A court enforcement officer issues a record of attachment; the employer must RESPOND WITHIN FIFTEEN DAYS and pay the seizable fraction MONTHLY to the DISTRIBUTING enforcement officer, who allocates it among the creditors - THE EMPLOYER NEVER APPORTIONS BETWEEN CREDITORS ITSELF. An employer that fails to respond or to pay over may be declared PERSONALLY LIABLE FOR THE DEBT. The scale applies to net pay AFTER social contributions AND AFTER THE WITHHELD INCOME TAX. 2026 scale, indexed by 0.9% by Decret n. 2025-1299 of 24 December 2025, monthly equivalents: to EUR 373.33 = 1/20; to EUR 727.50 = 1/10; to EUR 1,083.33 = 1/5; to EUR 1,435.83 = 1/4; to EUR 1,789.17 = 1/3; to EUR 2,150.83 = 2/3; above EUR 2,150.83 = entirely seizable. THE BANDS ARE CUMULATIVE, NOT SELECTIVE: the scale SLICES the pay, and reading a single fraction from the band containing total pay is wrong and materially over-deducts. EACH BAND LIMIT IS INCREASED BY EUR 145 PER MONTH (EUR 1,740 per year) PER DEPENDANT - the spouse or partner and each child or ascendant living with and supported by the employee, on evidence. | S21.G00.98.003 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No | No | No | No |  |  | DSN cahier technique de la norme NEODeS 2026.1, rubrique S21.G00.98.001 to .003 (Saisie administrative a tiers detenteur) - closest coded rubrique; an ordinary wage attachment is a net-pay deduction with no code value of its own - FR-compliance/00-official-sources/pdfs/dsn-cahier-technique-2026.1.pdf |

IT IS NOT CONFINED TO THE SEIZABLE FRACTION: it is payable from THE WHOLE SALARY above the absolutely protected amount, and it RANKS AHEAD OF EVERY OTHER CREDITOR including tax levies. This is the single most important ordering rule in French garnishment and the one most often implemented wrongly by treating maintenance as just another creditor inside the scale. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | No | No | No | No | No | No | No | No |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. | FR-055 | Absolutely protected amount | Fraction absolument insaisissable du salaire |  | Intermediary | EUR 651.69 PER MONTH FOR 2026 - the amount of the active-solidarity income for a single person with no dependants. Whatever the seizable-fraction scale produces, THE EMPLOYEE MUST RETAIN AT LEAST THIS SUM. IT IS NOT INCREASED FOR DEPENDANTS - only the scale bands are - and it yields ONLY to a maintenance order paid by direct payment. The full priority order is: (1) the absolutely protected amount reserved to the employee; (2) maintenance orders by direct payment; (3) tax levies; (4) ordinary garnishments within the seizable fraction; (5) the employer's own claims, limited to one tenth for advances. AN EMPLOYER THAT PAYS OUT OF ORDER, OR THAT EXCEEDS THE SEIZABLE FRACTION, BECOMES PERSONALLY LIABLE. | NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | NO CODED DSN RUBRIQUE ASSERTED. | FR-057 | Collective agreement identifier (IDCC) | Identifiant de convention collective (IDCC) |  | Flag | A FIRST-CLASS CONFIGURATION KEY ALONGSIDE THE ESTABLISHMENT IDENTIFIER, NOT AN INFORMATIONAL FIELD. The four-digit sector agreement identifier determines minimum pay scales by grade, the seniority bonus, the thirteenth month, the holiday bonus, notice periods, severance above the statutory floor, sector-specific premiums, the overtime premium rate where it departs from 25% / 50%, and the employer sick-pay top-up where it improves on the statutory scale. A THRESHOLD CROSSED UPWARD TAKES EFFECT ONLY AFTER IT HAS BEEN REACHED FOR FIVE CONSECUTIVE CALENDAR YEARS; A THRESHOLD CROSSED DOWNWARD TAKES EFFECT IMMEDIATELY. It decides the national housing aid fund rate and base (and therefore the Tdelta parameter of the general reduction), the employer flat deduction rate on overtime (20), the 8% social lump-sum levy on death-and-disability contributions (11), the training contribution rate (11), the mobility levy liability (11), the construction investment contribution (50), compulsory profit-sharing (50), the collective redundancy plan obligation (50) and the disabled-worker employment obligation (20).
